            (function (window, document) {
                if (window.wcfbq) return;
                window.wcfbq = (function () {
                    if (arguments.length > 0) {
                        var pixelId, trackType, contentObj;

                        if (typeof arguments[0] == 'string') pixelId = arguments[0];
                        if (typeof arguments[1] == 'string') trackType = arguments[1];
                        if (typeof arguments[2] == 'object') contentObj = arguments[2];

                        var params = [];
                        if (typeof pixelId === 'string' && pixelId.replace(/\s+/gi, '') != '' &&
                        typeof trackType === 'string' && trackType.replace(/\s+/gi, '')) {
                            params.push('id=' + encodeURIComponent(pixelId));
                            switch (trackType) {
                                case 'PageView':
                                case 'ViewContent':
                                case 'Search':
                                case 'AddToCart':
                                case 'InitiateCheckout':
                                case 'AddPaymentInfo':
                                case 'Lead':
                                case 'CompleteRegistration':
                                case 'Purchase':
                                case 'AddToWishlist':
                                    params.push('ev=' + encodeURIComponent(trackType));
                                    break;
                                default:
                                    return;
                            }

                            params.push('dl=' + encodeURIComponent(document.location.href));
                            if (document.referrer) params.push('rl=' + encodeURIComponent(document.referrer));
                            params.push('if=false');
                            params.push('ts=' + new Date().getTime());

                            if (typeof contentObj == 'object') {
                                for (var u in contentObj) {
                                    if (typeof contentObj[u] == 'object' && contentObj[u] instanceof Array) {
                                        if (contentObj[u].length > 0) {
                                            for (var y = 0; y < contentObj[u].length; y++) { contentObj[u][y] = (contentObj[u][y] + '').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/gi, '').replace(/\s+/gi, ' ').replace(/,/gi, '§'); }
                                            params.push('cd[' + u + ']=' + encodeURIComponent(contentObj[u].join(',').replace(/^/gi, '[\'').replace(/$/gi, '\']').replace(/,/gi, '\',\'').replace(/§/gi, '\,')));
                                        }
                                    }
                                    else if (typeof contentObj[u] == 'string')
                                        params.push('cd[' + u + ']=' + encodeURIComponent(contentObj[u]));
                                }
                            }

                            params.push('v=' + encodeURIComponent('2.7.19'));

                            var imgId = new Date().getTime();
                            var img = document.createElement('img');
                            img.id = 'fb_' + imgId, img.src = 'https://www.facebook.com/tr/?' + params.join('&'), img.width = 1, img.height = 1, img.style = 'display:none;';
                            document.body.appendChild(img);
                            window.setTimeout(function () { var t = document.getElementById('fb_' + imgId); t.parentElement.removeChild(t); }, 1000);
                        }
                    }
                });
            })(window, document);
        </script>

                        .find('.woocommerce-Price-currencySymbol').get()[0]).text();

                        content_ids: [ $(button).data('product_id') ],
                        content_type: 'product',
                        value: originalPrice,
                        currency: 'CAD'
                    });
                });
            });

<article id="post-3408">

	

<header class="entry-header clr">
	<h2 class="single-post-title entry-title" itemprop="headline">Futhark Runes: Symbols, Meanings and How to Use Them</h2><!-- .single-post-title -->
</header><!-- .entry-header -->



<div class="entry-content clr" itemprop="text">
			<div data-elementor-type="wp-post" data-elementor-id="3408" class="elementor elementor-3408" data-elementor-post-type="post">
						<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-cdf3b8c elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="cdf3b8c" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-87469e1" data-id="87469e1" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-86fcd21 elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="86fcd21" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
															<img decoding="async" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/elementor/thumbs/Elder-Futhark-Runes-Symbols-and-Meanings-wood-runes-riabx5vaa9v5cnemev65i9h4n4jwr0n8jm6758eiq4.jpg" title="Elder-Futhark-Runes-Symbols-and-Meanings-wood-runes" alt="Elder Futhark Runes: Symbols, Meanings, and how to Use Them" loading="lazy" />															</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-8afb636 elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="8afb636" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-4a3cded" data-id="4a3cded" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-f9a362d elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="f9a362d" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Updated and Expanded January 2023</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-38ef77a elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="38ef77a" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-1dfdf5e" data-id="1dfdf5e" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-aaf78e4 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="aaf78e4" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>When you think of the word rune it may bring up images of magic scrolls, Tolkein, and elven warriors. However, runes are not just the stuff of fantasy stories. They date back thousands of years – found in large numbers on ancient rock carvings in Scandinavia and around the world.</p>
<p>It’s a common misconception that runes are just letters – an alphabet from another language. While it’s true that Futhark is an old Germanic alphabet, it’s so much more than that.</p>
<p>The meaning of rune is “secret” or “mysterium”; each symbol has a deeper meaning above and beyond the sound it makes in language. A meaning that was traditionally held as a secret holy concept, “a formless and timeless idea”.</p>
<p>Keep reading to learn the origin of the runes, what each rune means, and how to use them as a divination tool or in rituals. </p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-ae5ba4f elementor-widget elementor-widget-menu-anchor" data-id="ae5ba4f" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="menu-anchor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
							<div class="elementor-menu-anchor" id="toc"></div>
						</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-b62a212 elementor-toc--minimized-on-tablet elementor-widget elementor-widget-table-of-contents" data-id="b62a212" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-settings="{&quot;headings_by_tags&quot;:[&quot;h2&quot;],&quot;exclude_headings_by_selector&quot;:&quot;.noTOC&quot;,&quot;collapse_subitems&quot;:&quot;yes&quot;,&quot;no_headings_message&quot;:&quot;No headings were found on this page.&quot;,&quot;marker_view&quot;:&quot;numbers&quot;,&quot;minimize_box&quot;:&quot;yes&quot;,&quot;minimized_on&quot;:&quot;tablet&quot;,&quot;hierarchical_view&quot;:&quot;yes&quot;,&quot;min_height&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;size&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;sizes&quot;:[]},&quot;min_height_tablet&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;size&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;sizes&quot;:[]},&quot;min_height_mobile&quot;:{&quot;unit&quot;:&quot;px&quot;,&quot;size&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;sizes&quot;:[]}}" data-widget_type="table-of-contents.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<div class="elementor-toc__header">
						<h4 class="elementor-toc__header-title">
				Table of Contents			</h4>
										<div class="elementor-toc__toggle-button elementor-toc__toggle-button--expand" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-controls="elementor-toc__b62a212" aria-expanded="true" aria-label="Open table of contents"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fas fa-chevron-down"></i></div>
				<div class="elementor-toc__toggle-button elementor-toc__toggle-button--collapse" role="button" tabindex="0" aria-controls="elementor-toc__b62a212" aria-expanded="true" aria-label="Close table of contents"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fas fa-chevron-up"></i></div>
					</div>
				<div id="elementor-toc__b62a212" class="elementor-toc__body elementor-toc__list-items--collapsible">
			<div class="elementor-toc__spinner-container">
				<i class="elementor-toc__spinner eicon-animation-spin eicon-loading" aria-hidden="true"></i>			</div>
		</div>
						</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-43fd6fb elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="43fd6fb" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-643c716" data-id="643c716" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-793a5a0 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="793a5a0" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">WHAT ARE RUNES?</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-c4875cd elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="c4875cd" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Runes are ancient alphabets. The word rune comes from an Old Norse term meaning a secret letter that was used for casting spells. Writing itself was often seen as magic by other peoples who had no writing systems of their own.</p>
<p>While there are many different types of runes, here I’ll be focusing on Elder Futhark.</p>
<p>The Elder Futhark runes are a set of 24 symbols that were used for writing in Scandinavia and other parts of Northern Europe from about 200-800 AD. The word &#8220;Futhark&#8221; is derived from the first six letters, which are called &#8220;Fehu,&#8221; &#8220;Uruz,&#8221; &#8220;Thurisaz,&#8221; &#8220;Ansuz,&#8221; &#8220;Raidho&#8221; and &#8220;Kennaz.&#8221;</p>
<p>The Elder Futhark (or sometimes just &#8220;Futhorc&#8221;) is the oldest form of runic alphabet. This writing system is believed to have originated from the Old Italic scripts: a variation of the North Italic (Etruscan or Raetic alphabets), or the Latin Alphabet itself.</p>
<p>The first runes were carved into wood, bone, or stone and then painted with various colours to make them more visible.</p>
<p>These ancient Norse runes have been found as far away as Iran, Turkey, England, and India. The first recorded use was found at a Viking settlement near Tängelgårda, Sweden. This runestone dates to around 400 AD but there are no written records that can tell us exactly when it was created.</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-57f7fa5 elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="57f7fa5" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-8b9fcd1" data-id="8b9fcd1" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-0bbf080 elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="0bbf080" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
																<a href="https://runedictionary.com/shop" target="_blank" rel="nofollow">
							<img decoding="async" width="1024" height="204" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/01/partner-offer-bindrune.webp" class="attachment-large size-large wp-image-9398" alt="" />								</a>
															</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-a7a6d59 elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="a7a6d59" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-a15d180" data-id="a15d180" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-e739c9c elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="e739c9c" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">RUNES IN MAGIC &amp; MYTHOLOGY</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-c393d30 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="c393d30" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Norse Mythology tells us that knowledge of runes and rune magic comes from Odin.</p>
<p>It is said that Odin received the mystery of the runes after hanging from Yggdrasil, the world tree, for nine days and nine nights. After sacrificing of himself, he was able to extract the wisdom of the runes. He then shared this gift with the rune vitki – sorcerers and magicians who used the power of the runes for divination and spellcasting.</p>
<p>There are countless stories within the Norse myths that describe the runes being used for various magical workings.</p>
<p>The most famous of these is the story in which Odin and Loki use runes to create a magical spear that will never miss its target.</p>
<p>The Norse myths also tell us about how each rune has an associated deity, animal or other entity with whom it&#8217;s connected . For example: Fehu represents cattle; Uruz symbolizes strength; Thurisaz is associated with the giants and so on.</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-2fe4370 elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="2fe4370" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-0d29a21" data-id="0d29a21" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-d229efe elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="d229efe" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">RUNE MEANINGS</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-d611961 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="d611961" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Unlike modern alphabets, runes have meanings and are tied to universal forces that change and evolve with time, making them just as relevant today as they were thousands of years ago. Rune meanings are very subjective and are often interpreted in different ways, depending on the working and past experiences of the reader.</p><p>Each rune contains three aspects:</p><ol><li style="list-style-type: none;"><ol><li>the symbol itself, what the shape represents</li><li>the name and what the word means, along with its letter value and sound</li><li>the energy and spirit embodied by the rune.</li></ol></li></ol><p>The 24 runes are organized into three sets of eight runes called aettir (singular aett), meaning families. The first runes of each of the three aettir are Fehu, Hagalaz, and Tiwaz, also called the Mother Runes.</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>
		</section>
				<section class="elementor-section elementor-top-section elementor-element elementor-element-00f878b elementor-section-boxed elementor-section-height-default elementor-section-height-default" data-id="00f878b" data-element_type="section" data-e-type="section">
						<div class="elementor-container elementor-column-gap-default">
					<div class="elementor-column elementor-col-100 elementor-top-column elementor-element elementor-element-31dc9ef" data-id="31dc9ef" data-element_type="column" data-e-type="column">
			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-a45f7b0 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="a45f7b0" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Scroll through the runes below to learn their meanings.</p>								</div>
				</div>

				<div class="elementor-widget-container">

<p>Raidho is the conscious decision to achieve something, and the discipline to carry it out. The rhythm found when your forward movement is in harmony with your purpose.</p>								</div>

				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-7efb562 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="7efb562" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Kenaz</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-e4009f5 elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="e4009f5" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
															<img decoding="async" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/elementor/thumbs/Kenaz-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon-riabx083nx6bgxmg528f8h9sc0mdvdg11736q2431w.png" title="Kenaz-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon" alt="Elder Futhark Kenaz Symbol" loading="lazy" />															</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-afe42a6 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="afe42a6" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: C / K</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;KEN-ahz&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: </strong>Torch, controlled energy, passion, creation &amp; transformation.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-e92f534 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="e92f534" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Kenaz is the torch: a fire lighting the way in the darkness. It is this same controlled fire used by the artisan to create, the cook to provide food, or that we gather around for warmth. This can also represent a metaphorical torch: a passion burning within, an inner light.</p>
<p>Kenaz is the fire of creation and transformation. It is a guiding light to expel outside influences and keep your inner passion aflame.</p>								</div>
				</div>

					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Gebo</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-ef817da elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="ef817da" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">

															<img decoding="async" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/elementor/thumbs/Gebo-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon-riabwycfa93qtpp6g1f63hqv58vnfz8kcxs7ri6vec.png" title="Gebo-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon" alt="Elder Futhark Gebo Symbol" loading="lazy" />															</div>

				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: G</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;GHEB-o&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: </strong>Gift, gratitude, exchange, receiving through sacrifice/offering.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-c1b24ef elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="c1b24ef" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Gebo represents a gift or an exchange. It is “the giver, the giving, the given, and that which is given to.” (Thorrson).</p>
<p>Giving is an energy that joins people together. Just as fire kindles fire, the act of giving breeds gratitude, which in turn creates more giving.</p>
<p>It’s a cycle, as important as it is to learn to give without expectations, so we must learn to receive graciously.</p>								</div>
				</div>

									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: H</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;HA-ga-lahz&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: </strong>Hail, temporary difficulties, changing of plans, delay.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-ebaf4a1 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="ebaf4a1" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Hagalaz represents hail &#8211; a temporary difficulty or delay. Disruption is a necessary part of life, it creates change and evolution.</p>
<p>It’s easy to get frustrated at a delay or lack of progress, but you’re better off using this time for rest and contemplation rather than to try to rush headlong through the storm.</p>								</div>

<p>Isa can be both external and internal &#8211; representing our own resistance to change: our ingrained patterns and old habits running deep within.</p>								</div>

									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: J / Y</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;YARE-a&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: </strong>Harvest, cycle, rewards for past efforts, reap what you have sown.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-00d7de2 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="00d7de2" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Jera represents the cycle of the year, or more specifically, the turning over of a new cycle. The end of the longest night and the slow returning of the light.</p>
<p>A more gentle change than Dagaz (which represents the daily cycle), Jera signals a reward for past actions. It is time to reap what we have sown.</p>								</div>

				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-e4ae283 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="e4ae283" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Eihwaz</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-2321384 elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="2321384" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
															<img decoding="async" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/elementor/thumbs/Eihwaz-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon-riabwxel3f2gi3qjlj0jizzejv0a8a4u0t4qa889kk.png" title="Eihwaz-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon" alt="Elder Futhark Eihwaz Symbol" loading="lazy" />															</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-7ae9657 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="7ae9657" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: E / I</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;AY-wahz&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: <span style="font-weight: 400;">Yew, wisdom, the mystery of life &amp; death, passing through a gateway.</span></strong></p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-e89c696 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="e89c696" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Eihwaz represents the yew &#8211; a tree of life and death. Yet this death is not final, it is a transition. A gateway through which we must pass to birth something new.</span></p>
<p><span style="font-weight: 400;">As with any new life, it requires sacrifice. The leaving behind of something old to make space for what is to come.</span></p>								</div>
				</div>

				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Perthro represents fate &#8211; but not in the modern sense of predetermination. The Norse idea of fate is more akin to what you’d think of as Karma. The sum of our past actions has created our current situation.</p>
<p>Perthro reminds us that where we are is a direct result of the choices we have made, which means that where we go from here is also in our own hands.</p>								</div>

				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-6df4a91 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="6df4a91" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Othala</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-832c76c elementor-widget elementor-widget-image" data-id="832c76c" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="image.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
															<img decoding="async" src="https://andreashelley.com/wp-content/uploads/elementor/thumbs/Othala-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon-riabx15xur7lsjl2zkn1sz18xehr32jrdbqo7c2ovo.png" title="Othala-Elder-Futhark-Rune-Icon" alt="Elder futhark othala symbol" loading="lazy" />															</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-321767b elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="321767b" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><strong>Phonetic Value</strong>: O</p>
<p><strong>Pronunciation</strong>: &#8220;OH-tha-la&#8221;</p>
<p><strong>Summary: <span style="font-weight: 400;">Ancestral property, wisdom, inherent talent, homecoming</span><span style="font-weight: 400;">.</span></strong></p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-7da3fd9 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="7da3fd9" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Othala is our birthright. It represents all the material and spiritual goods that we were each born into. These are the building blocks that we have to work with in life.</p>
<p>Othala is also a connection to the past, an inheritance. Our home, sense of belonging, and community.</p>								</div>
				</div>

			<div class="elementor-widget-wrap elementor-element-populated">
						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-b4b0e92 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="b4b0e92" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">HOW TO USE RUNES</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-f782960 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="f782960" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>There are many ways to use the runes. Runes are powerful tools for divination and ritual but you can also use them as <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">talismans</a> or <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/futhark-rune-necklace-sterling/">amulets</a>. Really there are so many ways that the runes can enrich your life.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-68b1c09 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="68b1c09" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Using Runes for divination and guidance</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-4ad5b20 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="4ad5b20" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Reading the runes is similar to doing tarot reading. You lay out the runes and interpret them based on their meanings and position in the spread.</p><p><a href="https://andreashelley.com/blog/rune-casting-guide-how-to-read-the-runes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Learn how to rune cast.</a></p><p>The most common tools for rune divination are <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">rune stones</a>: small rocks or pieces of wood or bone &#8211; inscribed with the rune symbols. You can make a set of rune stones yourself or <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">buy them</a> if you prefer.</p><p>These stones are placed in a bag, shaken up to mix them, and then drawn out one at a time and placed in a rune spread.</p><p>You can also get a set of runes in the form of a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/mini-rune-deck/">rune card deck</a> &#8211; which may be easier to carry around since they’re lighter and stack neatly together. You would shuffle these cards and lay them out one by one just as you would the rune stones.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-b3ad04a elementor-widget elementor-widget-spacer" data-id="b3ad04a" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="spacer.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
							<div class="elementor-spacer">
			<div class="elementor-spacer-inner"></div>
		</div>
						</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-0c5909c elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="0c5909c" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Using Runes in Ritual</h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-315ed67 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="315ed67" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Runes are powerful additions to rituals and meditations. Select one or several runes that encompass the intention of your ritual and use them alongside your other ritual tools.</p><p>Here are some suggestions for using runes in ritual:</p><ul><li>Hold a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">rune stone</a> in your hand during meditation</li><li>Place one or several <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/mini-rune-deck/">runes</a> on your altar before a ritual working.</li><li>Wear a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/futhark-rune-necklace-sterling/">rune pendant</a> during ritual or meditation.</li><li>Draw a rune symbol in a glass of water then focus on the intent of the rune as you drink it</li></ul><p>These are just a few of the many ways to use runes to enhance your rituals and meditations.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-c5ba229 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="c5ba229" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h3 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-large">Using Runes as a Talisman or Amulet </h3>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-4da2fc8 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="4da2fc8" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>Runes don’t have to be limited to ritual use, you can benefit from their energy every single day.</p><p>By carrying a rune as a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">talisman</a> or amulet, you can feel the energy of the symbol and be reminded of your intentions all day long.</p><p>Choose a rune that really speaks to your aspirations and inner purpose. You can also wear more than one to make an even more powerful combination.</p><p>You can tuck a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/rune-palm-stone/">rune stone</a> in your pocket or place it on your desk as a constant reminder of your intentions, or wear a <a href="https://andreashelley.com/shop/futhark-rune-necklace-sterling/">rune pendant</a> so you can carry the power of the runes with you everywhere you go.</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>
					</div>

						<div class="comment-entry">
						
							<div class="comment-content">
							<p>Yes you can absolutely do that. It&#8217;s not about special tools or materials, but about clearing your mind to focus on your intention as you trace the symbol. A pen and paper will work just fine for a short term goal or immediate purpose. You might want a more durable material if you&#8217;re going to be using it long term though.</p>

						<div class="clr"></div>

						<div class="comment-entry">
						
							<div class="comment-content">
							<p>Hi Julianne. There are a number of ways you could do this. You could translate a meaningful word into runes and place those symbols on the plaque or choose runes whose meanings represent what you&#8217;d like to embody in your home. My suggestions would be Othala (representing the ancestral home, inheritance and a sense of belonging), Gebo (for gratitude), and Wunjo (for joy and fulfilment). These runes could either be traced out in a row or combined together into a single symbol (a bindrune). Best of luck!</p>

	 * Displays toast message from storage, it is used when the user is redirected after login

	if ( window.sessionStorage ) {
		$( window ).on( 'tcb_after_dom_ready', () => {
			const message = sessionStorage.getItem( 'tcb_toast_message' );

				tcbToast( sessionStorage.getItem( 'tcb_toast_message' ), false );
				sessionStorage.removeItem( 'tcb_toast_message' );
			}

	}

	/**
	 * Displays toast message
	 *
	 * @param {string}   message  - message to display
	 * @param {Boolean}  error    - whether the message is an error or not
	 * @param {Function} callback - callback function to be called after the message is closed
	 */
	function tcbToast( message, error, callback ) {
		/* Also allow "message" objects */
		if ( typeof message !== 'string' ) {
			message = message.message || message.error || message.success;
		}
		if ( ! error ) {
			error = false;
		}
		TCB_Front.notificationElement.toggle( message, error ? 'error' : 'success', callback );
	}
